the section and appendix labels of its closing matter; unlabelled questions, remarks,
the integer-part definition and several sections received \texttt{saut:fs:} labels.
\item Printed once, with credit: the pairing criterion and its corollary
(\lbl{opa:as:thm:detect}, \lbl{opa:as:thm:ctiso}; the Baire proof kept as a further
route), the reconstruction (\lbl{opa:lem:divisible}, \lbl{odg:def:thm:multiplier},
\lbl{odg:thm:fractions}), Corollary~\ref{saut:fs:cor:finite-Gaussian}
(Theorem~\ref{saut:thm:torsion}; the source's route kept), and the definition of strong
maps (Section~\ref{saut:sec:hahn}, restated for general $k$ and $\Gamma$).
\item Stale against the current tree, corrected in place and marked \mergetag: the
source's strongness hypotheses (Proposition~\ref{saut:fs:prop:autostrong} and
Remark~\ref{saut:fs:rem:gaussianstrong}); its first question, now answered; its
second question, which is \lbl{opa:as:q:gaussian} and is not repeated as a new
question; and the second half of Remark~\ref{saut:fs:rem:valuation}. The source's
statements are kept beside the corrections.
\item Notation: the renamings of Section~\ref{saut:fs:sec:conventions}.
\item Bibliography: the entries \cite{Conway,Gonshor,KS,KKS} and the Artin--Schreier
notes \cite{Conrad} are shared by both sources, and \cite{Poonen,Lang,Wiki} were
added. The source's four repository entries (this report and the omnific-preserving,
omnific Diophantine and large-cardinal reports) are cited as collection paths.
\item Added in the merge: Proposition~\ref{saut:fs:prop:autostrong},
Remark~\ref{saut:fs:rem:gaussianstrong}, the consequences listed after
Proposition~\ref{saut:fs:prop:conjugacy} and Corollary~\ref{saut:fs:cor:standard-conj},
the identification $\mathcal H_{j_\theta}=P_\theta$, the assertion count of
Section~\ref{saut:fs:sec:verifier}, Section~\ref{saut:fs:sec:relations}, the status
notes, and items~23--27 of Section~\ref{saut:fs:sec:nonclaims}. Within
source~01, the title page, the abstract, Sections~\ref{saut:sec:position},
\ref{saut:sec:realforms}, \ref{saut:sec:questions} and~\ref{saut:sec:nonclaims} and
Appendix~\ref{saut:app:provenance} received pointers or status notes (marked
\mergetag\ outside the front matter); no mathematical statement of source~01 was
changed.
\end{enumerate}
The source's own comparison with the collection (its Section~13.2) was read against
the pinned tree. This report's nonconjugate real form and conjugacy criterion, the
phase twists and the omnific reconstruction are present there, as it says. The
automatic-strongness results were not in its tree.

\subsection{Source 03: Gaussian-omnific real forms}\label{saut:app:source03}

Sections~\ref{saut:gr:sec:introduction}--\ref{saut:gr:sec:closing} are built from a
third manuscript: the archive \texttt{gaussian\_omnific\_real\_forms}, manuscript~03 of
batch~34 (delivered in commit \texttt{a4611ad}), \emph{Gaussian-Omnific Real Forms and
Geometric Symmetrization: finite-action descent, coefficient-field classification, and
$2^{\mathfrak c}$ inequivalent integer-part real forms}, research manuscript prepared
with ChatGPT for Vladimir Reshetnikov, dated 23 September 2026, 24 pages, pinned to
repository commit \texttt{110efe9dea646a092a640838515af4ad2d7ee2e0}. It was placed in
commit \texttt{a7a435f} as an addition with local number~03, because it re-derives
source~02's normalization and count and adds an explicit inverse, a
characteristic-free descent and the arithmetic of the fixed rings. The pin contains the
placement of source~02 (\texttt{7d04483}) but not its write (\texttt{b8bbfee}); the
source's comparison read this report's article and README, which were then source~01
alone (Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:position}).

\paragraph{Files.} The manuscript source, its PDF, its delivery README and the checksum
manifest \texttt{SHA256SUMS.txt} of the delivery (seven entries, verified at placement)
are not shipped;
its text is Sections~\ref{saut:gr:sec:introduction}--\ref{saut:gr:sec:closing}. Shipped
byte-identical, with the prefix \texttt{03-gaussian-real-forms-}: the source and claim
audit \texttt{SOURCES.md} (report root), the build helper \texttt{build.sh} and the
verification program \texttt{verify.py} (\texttt{code/}), and the verification record
\texttt{verification.json} (\texttt{data/}). Those files still use the delivery names;
the README of this directory maps them.

\paragraph{What it contributes, and where the merge chose.}
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\arabic*)]
\item Placement: the source's Sections~1--13 and Appendices~A--B are
Sections~\ref{saut:gr:sec:introduction}--\ref{saut:gr:sec:closing}, after source~02's
closing section and before the appendices, so that no number of sources~01 and~02
changes. Its Section~1 is Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:introduction}; Sections~2--4 are
Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:inverse}; Section~5 is Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:orbit};
Section~6 is Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:gaussian}; Sections~7--8 are
Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:classify}; Sections~9--10 are Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:count};
Section~11 is Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:finite}; Section~12 is
Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:example}; Section~13 is Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:questions};
Section~14 and Appendices~A--B are Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:closing}.
\item Printed once, with credit: every statement that source~02 or the collection
already proves (the map of Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:map}); the source's genuinely
different proofs are kept as second routes (Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:relations}).
\item Labels: the source's labels are not carried (its results are either mapped to
existing labels or printed under new ones); every new label has the sub-prefix
\texttt{saut:gr:}.
\item Stale against the current tree: its statement that the collection lacked the
normalizer and the classification (Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:position}); its question on
abstractly surreal fixed fields, already answered (Question~\ref{saut:gr:q:abstract}); its
plain-field clause, weaker than Theorem~\ref{saut:fs:thm:No-criterion}
(Corollary~\ref{saut:gr:cor:standard}).
\item Notation: the renamings of Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:conventions}.
\item Bibliography: \cite{BKKS,KS,KKS,Conrad} are shared with the earlier sources;
\cite{StacksClosure,StacksTranscendence} were added. The source's two repository
entries (the omnific-preserving report and this report) are cited as collection paths.
\item Added in the merge: the statement map, the conventions table,
Remarks~\ref{saut:gr:rem:kernel} and~\ref{saut:gr:rem:routes}, the notes after
Corollary~\ref{saut:gr:cor:faithful}, Proposition~\ref{saut:gr:prop:leading},
Proposition~\ref{saut:gr:prop:split} and Proposition~\ref{saut:gr:prop:dio}, clause (v)
and the example in the proof of Corollary~\ref{saut:gr:cor:standard}, the status notes of
Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:questions}, the assertion count of
Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:verifier}, Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:relations} and items~26--31
of Section~\ref{saut:gr:sec:nonclaims}. In the earlier text: the title page and
abstract, Sections~\ref{saut:sec:position} and~\ref{saut:sec:kernels},
Theorem~\ref{saut:fs:thm:main-descent}, Questions~\ref{saut:fs:q:divisibility},
\ref{saut:fs:q:structure}, \ref{saut:fs:q:effective} and~\ref{saut:fs:q:formal}, and
Appendix~\ref{saut:app:provenance} received pointers or status notes, marked \mergetag\
outside the front matter; no mathematical statement of sources~01 and~02 was changed.
\end{enumerate}
The source's own comparison with the collection (its Appendix~B.1) was read against the
pinned tree: this report's article and README at \texttt{110efe9} are source~01 alone, as
it says, and the omnific-preserving report contains \lbl{opa:as:thm:detect},
\lbl{opa:as:thm:ctiso} and \lbl{opa:as:thm:gaussian}, which it credits.
